# Claim ledger — regroup — 2026-11-27

**Plan:** [plan-regroup-2026-11-27.md](plan-regroup-2026-11-27.md)
**Code:** `origin/main` at `ec5a7d4b` (#330). Production project `phoenix-cleanhouse`; Regroup's live Stripe account `acct_1GfryQKsstp3aqoP`.
**Targets:** `regroup/mobile`, `regroup/web` (Angular + SSR functions), `regroup/functions`; `regroup/portal` read where it bundles mobile code (merged, not released).
**Totals:** 1067 rows: 579 IMPLEMENTED · 65 PARTIAL · 45 MISSING · 121 CONTRADICTED · 77 STALE-DOC · 127 UNVERIFIABLE-HERE · 53 UNDOCUMENTED

Levels: L1 static read, L2 tests, L3 emulator, L4 test-mode/external call, L5 live state. Subagent rows are
L1 unless they say otherwise; the main session's `MS-` rows carry its L5 reads and its re-verification of every
BLOCKER and MUST-FIX lead. Where a subagent row and an `MS-` row disagree, the `MS-` row is the later, higher-level
evidence (for example LM-GL-26 vs MS-19, AM-U05 vs MS-13, and every "production unread" row vs MS-15/16/17).

Row IDs are stable within this run. Prefixes: MS main session; LM money docs; LP launch program; LC CLAUDE.md and
architecture; AM money path; AA/AR auth and retention; AS/AO store and operations.

## Part LC: CLAUDE.md files, rules and architecture docs (252 rows: 172 IMPLEMENTED, 14 PARTIAL, 1 MISSING, 41 CONTRADICTED, 8 STALE-DOC, 13 UNVERIFIABLE-HERE, 3 UNDOCUMENTED)

## Ledger LC — CLAUDE.md files, rules and architecture docs (regroup) — 2026-11-27

Worktree `lrp-regroup-2026-11-27` at `origin/main` = `ec5a7d4b` (#330). All paths are relative to the worktree root. Every row is L1 (static read) unless it says otherwise. No tests, emulators, gcloud or Stripe calls were run.

Shared facts used by many rows:

- **Function count.** `regroup/scripts/deploy-batched.sh` `ALL_FUNCTIONS` holds **70** names. Every name resolves to an export reachable from `regroup/functions/src/index.ts` (`stripeEvents` is the alias at `index.ts:51-54`). `regroup/functions/src/__tests__/deployList.test.ts` asserts the list equals the exported endpoints.
- **Callable regions.** No function sets `region:`; `regroup/functions/src/init.ts:13` sets only `setGlobalOptions({ cpu: 0.167, maxInstances: 2 })`. That means us-central1 everywhere, and the clients use the default region too: mobile `regroup/mobile/firebase-setup.ts:24` `getFunctions()`, portal `regroup/portal/src/adapter/firebase-setup.ts:29` `getFunctions(app)`, web `regroup/web/src/app/app.module.ts:220` `AngularFireFunctionsModule` with no REGION token. Hosting rewrites in `regroup/web/firebase.json` name `us-central1`. Every callable name used in mobile/web/portal source is exported. **No region drift at L1.**
- **Unit fields on the money path.** `payments.amount` is in cents from both writers: the webhook (`regroup/functions/src/webhooks/stripeWebhook.ts:411` `amount: paymentIntent.amount`) and the client's pending record (`regroup/mobile/src/screens/RentPayment/RentPaymentScreen.tsx:264-267` passes `amountInCents`). `rentOwed` writers: `rent/chargeResident.ts:149` (adds rent), `rent/linkIntakeRecord.ts:206` (moves an intake record's balance), `webhooks/stripeWebhook.ts:627` (payment decrement), `webhooks/refundRestore.ts:37` (refund restore), `scripts/reverseRentCharges.ts:80`. `stripeAccountId` writers: server `callable/payments.ts:717` (connect), `:668`/`:850` (delete), `accountDeletion/operatorRecords.ts:48`. **No client code writes it, but the rules let a house superAdmin write it** (LC-UND-01).

### A. Journey walk — operator, phone-first (iOS & Android)

Path traced: Landing → "manager" → Login (`InitialLandingForm.tsx:224-226`) → "Sign up" (`LoginFormView.tsx:77`) → SignUp with role
superAdmin renders `SignUpWebView` (`SignUp.tsx:273`) → "See plans" → system browser `https://regroup-app.com/pricing?from=app`
(`services/webBilling.ts:18-24`) → plan card → `/signup?houseType&tier&period&from=app` (`pricing-one.component.ts:117-125`) → web
creates the account (`createSuperAdmin`, `web/src/app/entities/User.ts:58-67`, writes `isSuperAdmin`/`potentialSuperAdmin: true`)
and checks out → `/checkout-return?result=success` (`signup.component.ts:97-103`) → landing page "Return to Regroup"
(`regroup-app://app/checkout-return…`) → app is signed out, link held as `pendingAppLink` → operator signs in → `resetToSignedInRoute`
→ `SUPER_ADMIN_INCOMPLETE` → Setup (`improved-navigation-service.ts` getUserState/handleRegularFlow) → OrgSetup plan check
(`hooks/useOrgSetupBlocker.ts`, re-checks on focus) → wizard → `createHouses` / `finishOperatorSetup` (claims via
`util/superAdminClaim.ts` → `setUserClaims`) → StripeSettings → `connectStripeAccount` → `Linking.openURL` (system browser,
`StripeSettingsScreen.tsx:254-266`) → return page → foreground refresh (`StripeSettingsScreen.tsx:234`, #294) → rent setup
(`RentSettingsScreen.tsx`, #307).

Stuck / drop-off points found (with evidence) are rows AA-07, AA-08, AA-09, AA-12, AA-13, AA-14 below.

---

### B. Journey walk — resident

Invitation email (`util/inviteEmails.ts:27-91`, click tracking off) → `https://regroup-app.com/invite#token=…` → iOS universal link
(AASA from `app-links.json`) or Android App Links (unverified until Play SHA, AA-12/17) or landing page fallback (AA-15) → app
`parseAppLink` → SignUp → `peekInvitation` prefill (`SignUp.tsx:185-225`) → createUser → `redeemInvitation` → force token refresh →
`writeInvitationMembership` (`SignUpForm.tsx:115-160`) → NewAccount → Main/Guest → SubscriptionGate (`hooks/useSubscriptionGate.ts`)
→ rent screens (money area).

Stuck points: AA-31 (email mismatch), AR-07 (silent email failure, no resend or copy-link), AA-12 (Android landing-page hop),
AA-15 (no deferred deep link after install).

---
